Install block:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force

        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_79_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_78_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_NEAR_MISS_COUNTERFACTUAL_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-78 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-79 until MLRT-78 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_79_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-79 Maximum-Optimized Differential Drift Controlled Offline ML Prompt-Selection Test Suite installed."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json remains ZIP delivery metadata only."
    Write-Host "Backup folder:"
    Write-Host $BACKUP_DIR
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Validation block:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT
    $env:PYTHONPATH = $PROJECT_ROOT

    $TESTS = @(
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt78_maximum_optimized_near_miss_counterfactual_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($TEST in $TESTS) {
        Write-Host ""
        Write-Host "RUNNING:"
        Write-Host $TEST
        python $TEST
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "test failed: $TEST"
        }
    }

    python -m py_compile "tests\test_rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "py_compile failed for MLRT-79 test"
    }

    python -m py_compile "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\source_surface\minimal_non_runtime_harness_stub.py"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "py_compile failed for MLRT source file"
    }

    python -m py_compile "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\lab_non_runtime_router_evaluation\candidate_evaluation_harness_interface.py"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "py_compile failed for candidate_evaluation_harness_interface.py"
    }

    python -m py_compile "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\lab_non_runtime_router_evaluation\deterministic_runner_skeleton.py"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "py_compile failed for deterministic_runner_skeleton.py"
    }

    python -m py_compile "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\lab_non_runtime_router_evaluation\lab_self_validation_gate.py"
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "py_compile failed for lab_self_validation_gate.py"
    }

    $MLRT_BOX = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability"
    $MLRT_PY_FILES = @(Get-ChildItem -Path $MLRT_BOX -Recurse -Filter "*.py" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName })

    if ($MLRT_PY_FILES.Count -ne 1) {
        throw "MLRT-79 boundary violation: expected exactly one MLRT Python source file."
    }

    if (($MLRT_PY_FILES[0] -replace "\\", "/") -notmatch "source_surface/minimal_non_runtime_harness_stub.py$") {
        throw "MLRT-79 boundary violation: unexpected MLRT Python source file."
    }

    $LAB_BOX = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\lab_non_runtime_router_evaluation"
    $ALLOWED_LAB_PY = @(
        "candidate_evaluation_harness_interface.py",
        "deterministic_runner_skeleton.py",
        "lab_self_validation_gate.py"
    )

    $LAB_PY_NAMES = @(Get-ChildItem -Path $LAB_BOX -Recurse -Filter "*.py" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { $_.Name } | Sort-Object)

    if ($LAB_PY_NAMES.Count -ne 3) {
        throw "MLRT-79 boundary violation: expected exactly three LAB Python files."
    }

    foreach ($NAME in $LAB_PY_NAMES) {
        if ($ALLOWED_LAB_PY -notcontains $NAME) {
            throw "MLRT-79 boundary violation: unauthorized LAB Python file found: $NAME"
        }
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not exist in project root after install."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "VALIDATION OK: rss_mlrt79_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_v1"
    Write-Host "CONTRACT_TEST_OK: MLRT-79 Maximum-Optimized Differential Drift Controlled Offline ML Prompt-Selection Test Suite v1, executed a 64-case maximum-optimized differential drift in-memory offline prompt-selection test suite after MLRT-78 freeze; MLRT-78 accepted the MLRT-77 64-case near-miss counterfactual suite as good but validation-only evidence and identified differential drift as the next correction; MLRT-79 follows the standing rule that future real ML prompt-selection suites use the maximum optimized number of coherent non-duplicate cases; MLRT-79 passed 64/64 differential drift cases across eight balanced audit families, with four drift-stability pairs per family and two wording/context-shift variants per pair; coverage protection passed: 64/64 unique case IDs, 64/64 unique user requests, 32/32 differential drift pairs represented, 8/8 families represented with 8 cases each, 32 governed offline-review-only stable cases, 32 containment/no-authority stable cases, no duplicate filler, no forbidden selected routes, no persistence, no dataset files, no training data, no prompt loading, and no route authority; cumulative controlled offline prompt-selection coverage is now 282/282 cases across eight real test suites, but this remains validation-only evidence and not reliability, maturity, production-readiness, training, model-improvement, or runtime-route-authority evidence; preserved the final goal that ML must be tested for helping prompt selection in router prompt logic; no runtime routing, no route authority, no router prompt logic modification, no prompt loading, no provider calls, no embeddings, no persistence, no report persistence, no training-data intake, no dataset creation, no model training, no model calibration, no model improvement, no gold registry write, no registry mutation, no runtime Pilot, no Copilot behavior, critical boundary error budget zero."

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force

        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_80_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt80_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_79_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-79 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-80 until MLRT-79 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_80_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt80_maximum_optimized_differential_drift_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-80 Maximum-Optimized Differential Drift Controlled Offline ML Prompt-Selection Test Suite Result Review Gate installed."
    Write-Host "MLRT-80 adds 0 new cases and reviews MLRT-79 only for continued offline testing."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json remains ZIP delivery metadata only."
    Write-Host "Backup folder:"
    Write-Host $BACKUP_DIR
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

    if ($LAB_PY_NAMES.Count -ne 3) {

        if ($ALLOWED_LAB_PY -notcontains $NAME) {

        }
    }

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt81_metamorphic_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_81_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_REGRESSION_METAMORPHIC_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt81_maximum_optimized_regression_metamorphic_consistency_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_80_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_DIFFERENTIAL_DRIFT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-80 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-81 until MLRT-80 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_81_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_REGRESSION_METAMORPHIC_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt81_maximum_optimized_regression_metamorphic_consistency_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-81 metamorphic consistency suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt81_metamorphic_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-81 adds 64 real in-memory offline metamorphic consistency cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"

    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT
    $env:PYTHONPATH = $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $FIX_ZIP)) {
        throw "Missing freeze-form intake fix ZIP at: $FIX_ZIP"

import json
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from kanda_reasoner_app.freeze_hint_intake import contract

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(r"E:\kanda_reasoner")
FIX_ZIP = Path(r"E:\kanda_reasoner_delete_after_daily_work\mlrt82_review_gate_v1_freeze_form_intake_fix.zip")

EXPECTED_TITLE = "Routing Signal Scorer MLRT-82 Maximum-Optimized Regression Metamorphic Consistency Controlled Offline ML Prompt-Selection Test Suite Result Review Gate v1"
EXPECTED_FEATURE_ID = "rss_mlrt82_maximum_optimized_regression_metamorphic_consistency_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate_v1"
EXPECTED_SANDBOX = "SANDBOX_RSS_MLRT82_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_REGRESSION_METAMORPHIC_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE_V1_VALIDATION_OK"

def fail(message: str) -> None:
    raise SystemExit(message)

if not FIX_ZIP.exists():
    fail(f"Fix ZIP not found: {FIX_ZIP}")

with zipfile.ZipFile(FIX_ZIP, "r") as z:
    if "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json" not in z.namelist():
        fail("KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json not found in fix ZIP.")
    raw_hint = json.loads(z.read("KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json").decode("utf-8-sig"))

title = str(raw_hint.get("feature_title") or "").strip()
feature_id = str(raw_hint.get("feature_id") or "").strip()
evidence = str(raw_hint.get("validation_evidence_summary") or "")

if title != EXPECTED_TITLE:
    fail("Feature title mismatch in corrected freeze hint.")

if feature_id != EXPECTED_FEATURE_ID:
    fail("Feature ID mismatch in corrected freeze hint.")

for marker in ("VALIDATION OK:", "CONTRACT_TEST_OK:", EXPECTED_SANDBOX):
    if marker not in evidence:
        fail(f"Corrected freeze hint is still missing validation marker: {marker}")

source_signature = {
    "source_path": str(FIX_ZIP),
    "source_name": FIX_ZIP.name,
    "source_kind": "mlrt82_freeze_form_intake_fix_zip",
}

record = contract.save_freeze_hint_record(
    PROJECT_ROOT,
    raw_hint,
    source_signature=source_signature,
)

loaded = contract.load_latest_freeze_hint_record(PROJECT_ROOT)
if not loaded.get("ok"):
    fail("Latest freeze hint record was saved but could not be loaded: " + json.dumps(loaded, indent=2))

latest = loaded.get("record") or {}
form_inputs = latest.get("form_inputs") or {}
saved_title = str(form_inputs.get("feature_title") or "").strip()
saved_evidence = str(form_inputs.get("validation_evidence_summary") or "")

if saved_title != EXPECTED_TITLE:
    fail("Saved freeze form title mismatch.")

for marker in ("VALIDATION OK:", "CONTRACT_TEST_OK:", EXPECTED_SANDBOX):
    if marker not in saved_evidence:
        fail(f"Saved freeze form validation evidence is missing marker: {marker}")

latest_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "project_freeze_after_update" / "freeze_hint_intake" / "latest_freeze_hint.json"

print("")
print("FORM FILL OK")
print("Corrected MLRT-82 freeze hint intake saved.")
print("")
print("Latest freeze hint:")
print(latest_path)
print("")
print("Feature title:")
print(saved_title)
print("")
print("Validation marker:")
print("VALIDATION OK: " + EXPECTED_FEATURE_ID)
print("")
print("OPEN FREEZE FEATURE AFTER UPDATE NOW")
print("Then click: Preview Freeze Entry")
print("Expected preview: Writable YES / Validation OK")
print("Then click: Confirm and Write")
print("")
print("Required final output:")
print("LOCAL FREEZE WRITE OK")
print("FREEZE_MEMORY_STATUS: OK")
print("")
'@

    Set-Content -Path $FORM_FILL_SCRIPT -Value $PY -Encoding UTF8

    python $FORM_FILL_SCRIPT
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {

    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "FORM FILL OK"
    Write-Host "Now open Freeze Feature After Update > Preview Freeze Entry > Confirm and Write."
    Write-Host ""
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "FORM FILL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt83_semantic_collision_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_83_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt83_maximum_optimized_semantic_collision_disambiguation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_82_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_REGRESSION_METAMORPHIC_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-82 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-83 until MLRT-82 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_83_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt83_maximum_optimized_semantic_collision_disambiguation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-83 semantic collision disambiguation suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt83_semantic_collision_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-83 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt84_review_gate_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_84_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt84_maximum_optimized_semantic_collision_disambiguation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_83_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-83 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-84 until MLRT-83 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_84_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt84_maximum_optimized_semantic_collision_disambiguation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-84 semantic collision result review gate installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt84_review_gate_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-84 adds 0 new real cases and reviews MLRT-83 64/64 semantic collision disambiguation coverage."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt85_ambiguity_saturation_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_85_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AMBIGUITY_SATURATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt85_maximum_optimized_ambiguity_saturation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_84_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_SEMANTIC_COLLISION_DISAMBIGUATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-84 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-85 until MLRT-84 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_85_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AMBIGUITY_SATURATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt85_maximum_optimized_ambiguity_saturation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-85 ambiguity saturation suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt85_ambiguity_saturation_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-85 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt86_review_gate_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_86_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AMBIGUITY_SATURATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt86_maximum_optimized_ambiguity_saturation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_85_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AMBIGUITY_SATURATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-85 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-86 until MLRT-85 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_86_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AMBIGUITY_SATURATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt86_maximum_optimized_ambiguity_saturation_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-86 ambiguity saturation result review gate installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt86_review_gate_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-86 adds 0 new real cases and reviews MLRT-85 64/64 ambiguity saturation coverage."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt88_review_gate_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_88_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STATE_TRANSITION_EVIDENCE_RECOGNITION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt88_maximum_optimized_state_transition_evidence_recognition_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_87_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STATE_TRANSITION_EVIDENCE_RECOGNITION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-87 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-88 until MLRT-87 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_88_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STATE_TRANSITION_EVIDENCE_RECOGNITION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt88_maximum_optimized_state_transition_evidence_recognition_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-88 state-transition evidence recognition result review gate installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt88_review_gate_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-88 adds 0 new real cases and reviews MLRT-87 64/64 state-transition evidence recognition coverage."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf

$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_89_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_TEMPORAL_RECENCY_ARBITRATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt89_maximum_optimized_temporal_recency_arbitration_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_88_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STATE_TRANSITION_EVIDENCE_RECOGNITION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-88 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-89 until MLRT-88 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_89_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_TEMPORAL_RECENCY_ARBITRATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt89_maximum_optimized_temporal_recency_arbitration_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-89 temporal recency arbitration suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt89_temporal_recency_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-89 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {

    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

Install:

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt91_user_correction_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_91_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt91_maximum_optimized_user_correction_evidence_recovery_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_90_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_TEMPORAL_RECENCY_ARBITRATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-90 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-91 until MLRT-90 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_91_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt91_maximum_optimized_user_correction_evidence_recovery_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-91 user-correction evidence recovery suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt91_user_correction_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-91 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt92_review_gate_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_92_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt92_maximum_optimized_user_correction_evidence_recovery_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_91_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-91 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-92 until MLRT-91 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_92_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt92_maximum_optimized_user_correction_evidence_recovery_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite_result_review_gate.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-92 user-correction evidence recovery result review gate installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt92_review_gate_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-92 adds 0 new real cases and reviews MLRT-91 64/64 user-correction evidence recovery coverage."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_93_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_CURRENT_FEATURE_FREEZE_INTAKE_PRECEDENCE_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt93_maximum_optimized_current_feature_freeze_intake_precedence_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_92_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_USER_CORRECTION_EVIDENCE_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-92 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-93 until MLRT-92 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_93_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_CURRENT_FEATURE_FREEZE_INTAKE_PRECEDENCE_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt93_maximum_optimized_current_feature_freeze_intake_precedence_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-93 current-feature freeze-intake precedence suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt93_current_feature_precedence_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-93 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."
    Write-Host ""
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    Clear-Host
}
catch {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL FAILED:"
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    Write-Host ""
    Read-Host "Press Enter after copying this error"
    Clear-Host
    Read-Host "Press Enter again to clear"
    Clear-Host
}

$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt97_preview_write_boundary_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf

$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_97_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_PREVIEW_VERSUS_WRITE_BOUNDARY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt97_maximum_optimized_preview_versus_write_boundary_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_96_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_FREEZE_EXPOSURE_STATUS_RECOVERY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-96 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-97 until MLRT-96 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_97_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_PREVIEW_VERSUS_WRITE_BOUNDARY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt97_maximum_optimized_preview_versus_write_boundary_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "INSTALL OK: MLRT-97 preview-versus-write boundary suite installed."
    Write-Host "Compact ZIP name used: mlrt97_preview_write_boundary_v1_patch.zip"
    Write-Host "MLRT-97 adds 64 real in-memory offline prompt-selection cases."
    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."

$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."

    $EXPECTED = @(

        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",

    }

        if (Test-Path $DST) {

        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null

try {

try {

    )

$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_103_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_FREEZE_INDEX_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt103_maximum_optimized_freeze_index_consistency_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_102_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_WRITTEN_PATH_INTEGRITY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-102 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-103 until MLRT-102 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",

        "tests\test_rss_mlrt103_maximum_optimized_freeze_index_consistency_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."

$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")

$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_105_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_AI_SEND_EXPOSURE_ALIGNMENT_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt105_maximum_optimized_ai_send_exposure_alignment_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"

        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {

        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_104_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_FREEZE_INDEX_CONSISTENCY_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-104 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-105 until MLRT-104 is installed and frozen."

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",

        "tests\test_rss_mlrt105_maximum_optimized_ai_send_exposure_alignment_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."

try {

        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {

        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {

    $EXPECTED = @(

        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",

$PROJECT_ROOT = "E:\kanda_reasoner"
$PATCH_NAME = "mlrt109_next_step_arbitration_v1_patch"

$PROJECT_NAME = Split-Path $PROJECT_ROOT -Leaf
$DRIVE_ROOT = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($PROJECT_ROOT)
$WORK_DIR = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PROJECT_NAME + "_delete_after_daily_work")

$ROOT_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $DRIVE_ROOT ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
$EXTRACT_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR $PATCH_NAME
$BACKUP_DIR = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ("backup_" + $PATCH_NAME + "_" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))

try {
    Set-Location $PROJECT_ROOT

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_DIR)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $WORK_DIR -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        Copy-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Destination $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        if (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP) {
            Remove-Item -Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP -Force
        }
    }

    if (-not (Test-Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP)) {
        throw "zip is not in root of drive:\ where project is"
    }

    if (Test-Path $ROOT_PATCH_ZIP) {
        throw "Root-drive ZIP copy still exists after staging."
    }

    if (Test-Path $EXTRACT_DIR) {
        Remove-Item $EXTRACT_DIR -Recurse -Force
    }

    Expand-Archive -Path $WORK_PATCH_ZIP -DestinationPath $EXTRACT_DIR -Force

    $EXPECTED = @(
        "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_109_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STARTUP_HANDOFF_NEXT_STEP_ARBITRATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt109_maximum_optimized_startup_handoff_next_step_arbitration_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    foreach ($REL in $EXPECTED) {
        $CHECK = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        if (-not (Test-Path $CHECK)) {
            throw "Expected file missing inside patch ZIP: $REL"
        }
    }

    $PREV_DOC = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_108_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STARTUP_FREEZE_CONTEXT_PROPAGATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE_RESULT_REVIEW_GATE.md"
    if (-not (Test-Path $PREV_DOC)) {
        throw "Missing MLRT-108 prerequisite document. Do not install MLRT-109 until MLRT-108 is installed and frozen."
    }

    $FILES = @(
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\box_manifest.json",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\README.md",
        "kanda_reasoner_app\routing_signal_scorer\mlrt_non_runtime_candidate_reliability\MLRT_109_MAXIMUM_OPTIMIZED_STARTUP_HANDOFF_NEXT_STEP_ARBITRATION_CONTROLLED_OFFLINE_ML_PROMPT_SELECTION_TEST_SUITE.md",
        "tests\test_rss_mlrt109_maximum_optimized_startup_handoff_next_step_arbitration_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    )

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BACKUP_DIR -Force | Out-Null

    foreach ($REL in $FILES) {
        $SRC = Join-Path $EXTRACT_DIR $REL
        $DST = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT $REL

        if (Test-Path $DST) {
            $BACKUP_FILE = Join-Path $BACKUP_DIR $REL
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $BACKUP_FILE -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item $DST $BACKUP_FILE -Force
        }

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path $DST -Parent) -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item $SRC $DST -Force
    }

    $TEST109 = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "tests\test_rss_mlrt109_maximum_optimized_startup_handoff_next_step_arbitration_controlled_offline_ml_prompt_selection_test_suite.py"
    if (-not (Test-Path $TEST109)) {
        throw "Install failed: MLRT-109 test file is missing after copy."
    }

    $ROOT_HINT = Join-Path $PROJECT_ROOT "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json"
    if (Test-Path $ROOT_HINT) {
        throw "KANDA_FREEZE_HINT.json must not be installed into project root."
    }

    Write-Host ""

    Write-Host "No runtime routing, route authority, router prompt logic modification, prompt loading, provider calls, embeddings, persistence, training, registry mutation, runtime Pilot, or Copilot behavior was added."

$WORK_PATCH_ZIP = Join-Path $WORK_DIR ($PATCH_NAME + ".zip")
